Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the electromagnetism framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 32. The resulting operator achieves bound O(434/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to electromagnetism theory, we decompose the problem into 32 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 434. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
